its deterministic synthetic provenance. Seed `799940857` then passed the
prompt-size boundary and completed both the initial decision and one bounded
clarification call. The model again asked for a fresh cited observation after
that synthetic sensor observation had been presented. Its captured question
was “I do not have a fresh, cited observation that establishes the current
condition. Could you provide a fresh observation?” The initial and follow-up
responses had the same response digest, while the follow-up prompt had its own
receipt and digest. No action was authorized or simulated, and the cycle is
incomplete; this is not evidence of learning or task performance. The run's
source provenance remained stable across its inference calls, but the campaign
preflight predates the final reduction of decision-time evidence and therefore
does not validate that final source revision. See the [plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d2154e298ead49cb8472/plan_v1.json),
[owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d2154e298ead49cb8472/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json),
and [run-bound summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d2154e298ead49cb8472/cycle0_reduced_evidence_result_summary_v1.json),
which identifies the external failure artifact by path and SHA-256. A fresh
plan and preflight must bind the current source before another inference run.

A CPU-only replay of the exact companion bridge and cognition owner exposed a
second boundary: ask capture allowed up to 1,024 UTF-8 bytes, while the owner
rejects questions longer than 1,200 characters. JSON escaping can expand a
bounded ask beyond that owner limit. The runner now measures the assembled
clarification question before calling the owner and records an explicit
no-follow-up status when it exceeds the shared owner limit. The new
[decision-prompt preflight](../../work/preflight_rootless_embodied_decision_prompt.py)
uses a fake invoker that stops at the request boundary and persists only
digests, sizes, and current fact IDs. It measured 23,451 characters for the
initial request and 23,581 for a worst-case JSON-escaped clarification request,
both below the 24,576-character owner budget. The worst-case Unicode ask that
fit both limits was 104 source bytes and produced a 1,190-character owner
question. The report verifies complete, unchanged source provenance during
preview and records no backend entry, inference, authority, or action.

A final two-cycle plan binds the source revision after adding explicit
campaign/seed/model-identity binding to the decision-prompt preflight.
`verify-plan` matched the current implementation and both model identities.
The CPU-only owner preflight verified seed `799940892`, transfer probes
`[100, 309, 743]`, zero exact overlap with selected history, and a
22,172-character transfer prompt under the same limit. The embodied-decision
preflight names this exact plan digest and cycle ID, records stable source
provenance, and reports 23,451- and 23,581-character requests for the initial
and worst-case clarification turns. Both reports record no inference and no
learning. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-03b4e89ad31c4aa99214/plan_v1.json),
[owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-03b4e89ad31c4aa99214/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json),
[plan verification](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-03b4e89ad31c4aa99214/owner_preflight_verification_v1.json),
and [plan-bound embodied-decision prompt preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-03b4e89ad31c4aa99214/embodied_decision_prompt_preflight_v1.json).
Hermes inference remains paused while the GPU is in use.

The next decision-path revision treats an admitted ask as an active-sensing
trigger. A bounded follow-up now receives a newly timestamped second synthetic
sensor sample (`hazard_status: clear`) instead of repeating the original
unknown-hazard reading. The clarification explicitly identifies this as a
deterministic test fixture and says it is not a physical-world observation.
The runner records the refresh source ID, schema, digest, timestamp, and
provenance in the bounded clarification metadata; this metadata carries no
authority. A plan-bound CPU prompt preflight captured the actual companion
bridge requests: 23,451 characters initially and 23,643 for the worst-case
bounded clarification. Both fit the 24,576-character owner limit. The report
binds campaign `rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000001`, plan digest
`12d96994593175c000111f64fac04526df5bd290140ed5bd34bf8c2925f68598`, and a
stable source digest. It records no model inference, backend entry, authority,
or action. This validates the synthetic evidence-refresh path and its request
budget only; it does not establish that a model uses new evidence correctly,
that sensing works in a physical environment, or that learning occurred. See
the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000001/plan_v1.json),
[plan-bound prompt preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000001/embodied_prompt_preflight_v1.json),
and [preflight implementation](../../work/preflight_rootless_embodied_decision_prompt.py).

The first Hermes cycle reached both pre-action calls. The follow-up receipt
bound a different prompt digest, a different current projection, and a changed
current fact set after the synthetic sensor refresh. Hermes nevertheless
returned the same validated ask response as on the initial turn; the runner
recorded an incomplete cycle and executed no action. The follow-up prompt was
then revised to say that the fresh-observation request had been fulfilled,
prohibit repeating that request, and permit a different specific evidence
request or abstention. This wording remained under the owner cap and was bound
to a new campaign seeded from the prior cycle's recovery state (403 files,
5,960,661 bytes). The second Hermes cycle again received distinct prompt and
current-projection digests, but returned the identical validated ask response
digest across both turns. Both cycles persisted two cognition observations,
and neither executed an action. This is evidence that the bounded refresh is
delivered and retained while this Hermes configuration did not change its
response under the prompt revision. It is not evidence of learning or physical
sensing. See the [follow-up outcome report](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000003/refresh_followup_outcome_v1.json),
[revised campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000003/plan_v1.json),
[owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000003/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json),
and [prompt preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000003/embodied_prompt_preflight_v1.json).
The two external cycle-failure artifacts are identified by SHA-256 in the
outcome report.

The runner now persists the current projection digest and current fact IDs in
each bounded pre-action decision diagnostic. A follow-up ask is classified as
`repeated_ask_after_new_current_projection` only when its response digest is
unchanged and the projection digest changed. The classification does not
authorize an action. The next Hermes cycle produced that exact outcome: its
initial and follow-up turns had different projection digests and fact sets,
but the same response digest. Two cognition observations were retained and
the cycle stopped before action. The plan, model identity, CPU owner preflight,
and embodied prompt preflight verify against this runner revision. See the
[projection-aware outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000004/projection_aware_repeat_ask_outcome_v1.json),
[campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000004/plan_v1.json),
[owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000004/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json),
and [prompt preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000004/embodied_prompt_preflight_v1.json).

The latest incomplete cycle also retained both pre-action ask turns as
`ResidentCognitionObservation` records in its recovery state. Their output
digests match the run's initial and follow-up ask diagnostics, and both records
bind verified inference receipt IDs/digests. A new sequential plan now seeds
from that cycle's history and recovery state: 29 history records, 29 receipts,
and the 399-file recovery-state boundary. The existing runner recovery copier
copied this state into a temporary isolated destination and reproduced the
boundary digest exactly. A CPU-only owner preflight for the next transfer seed
passed with zero exact probe overlap and a 22,172-character prompt. This
establishes durable observation and exact-copy continuity; it does not show
that later cognition attends to these observations or learns from them. The
pre-action decision still explicitly withholds developmental history. See the
[continuation plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d1ed9c4eb2bb4ff0a056/plan_v1.json),
[recovery-state copy verification](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d1ed9c4eb2bb4ff0a056/recovery_seed_copy_verification_v1.json),
[owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d1ed9c4eb2bb4ff0a056/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json),
and [preflight verification](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-d1ed9c4eb2bb4ff0a056/owner_preflight_verification_v1.json).


The no-action continuation revision was exercised in campaign 05. A lineage-verified pre-action ask left authority closed and skipped consequence writeback, while the cycle continued into independently scored task-family cognition. The completed cycle created no consequence record, then persisted one score-bound acquisition record with 0 correct and 3 incorrect answers. Transfer qualification was withheld and no learning or improvement claim is supported. The controlled history intervention also failed semantic qualification, and the direct action-decision intervention was skipped because no consequence record existed. This is useful negative evidence: continuation permits measurement without converting an ask into action, but this task/model combination did not demonstrate competence.

The planned cycle 1 CPU preflight could not use the newly accumulated cycle 0 history: the plan verifier requires the original seed boundary, and appending cycle 0 changed that boundary. The campaign boundary verifier also requires all planned cycle roots. The plan was left frozen rather than rebound after observing outcomes. Resume with a new sequential plan that explicitly seeds from cycle 0 if testing whether the failed examples alter later cognition is still desired. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000005/plan_v1.json), [cycle 0 outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000005/no_action_cognition_continuation_outcome_v1.json), and cycle 1 preflight rejection recorded in that outcome.


Campaign 06 starts from the completed cycle 0 history boundary and was verified without inference. Its CPU owner preflight confirms the score-bound 0/3 acquisition record is present in family-history ranking, but it appears at rank 13 (zero-based 12), beyond the four-record owner limit. The prompt binder therefore uses an older record with correct examples. Across 16 alternate task seeds the binder still selects one identical record set. Held-out transfer probes remain disjoint from all selected historical probes, and the prompt is within its bound. This shows durable storage alone is not enough: the current retrieval policy does not surface this recent negative task feedback in the bound prompt. It does not show what Hermes would attend to or whether performance would change. The next retrieval improvement should deliberately expose score-bound negative examples with their incorrect labels and existing no-imitation instruction intact, then prove delivery with a newly pinned CPU preflight before any GPU experiment. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000006/plan_v1.json), [verified owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000006/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [retrieval outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000006/history_retrieval_projection_outcome_v1.json).


Campaign 09 applies a retrieval-policy revision to the score-bound 0/3 record. Family ranking now considers distinct evaluator-scored input coverage (correct, incorrect, or abstained) before exact-model and probe tie-breaks, with correct-example coverage only as the final tie-breaker. The ranking trace now reports score coverage, usable inputs, and probe overlap under the correct labels. A new CPU-only preflight, pinned after the change, selected the recent all-incorrect record at rank 1 and bound all three incorrect examples into the planned transfer prompt. Its projection verifies the existing no-imitation instruction and marks this material historical-only, not current truth, and without authority. The held-out transfer probes remain disjoint and the prompt remains within its bound.

The acquisition prompt in this preflight selected an older positive record and contained no negative examples; the transfer prompt carried the new failure record. This is delivery evidence for the transfer pathway only. The paired positive-example ablation correctly reports that no positive examples were present, and its verifier now treats that as an absent ablation rather than a malformed report. No inference was run, so attention, behavior change, learning, and performance improvement remain unestablished. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000009/plan_v1.json), [verified CPU preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000009/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [delivery outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000009/score_bound_negative_history_delivery_outcome_v1.json).


Campaign 10 was the first pinned verification of the score-bound negative-history retrieval revision. The owner now ranks distinct scored input coverage before correct-example coverage, and the trace labels usable coverage and probe overlap accurately. The verifier independently reconstructs and compares the ranking trace and negative-example delivery diagnostic. It confirms that the cycle 0 0/3 record ranks first, is selected by the owner and transfer prompt binder, and contributes all three labeled incorrect examples with the no-imitation instruction in the prompt. The held-out transfer probes are disjoint from selected history, and the prompt remains within the input bound. The acquisition prompt still selects positive history and contains no negative examples, so delivery is established for the planned transfer pathway only. No model inference occurred; attention, adaptation, learning, and performance improvement remain unproven. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000010/plan_v1.json), [verified owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000010/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [negative-history delivery outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000010/negative_history_policy_retrieval_outcome_v2.json).


Campaign 11 re-pinned the retrieval revision after restoring read compatibility for history records tagged with policy v9. The final verifier reconstructs and compares the ranking trace and negative-example delivery fields. It confirms the recent 0/3 record is selected into the transfer prompt with three incorrect examples and the no-imitation instruction, while held-out transfer probes remain disjoint and prompt length is within the bound. Both the acquisition and transfer prompts contain the three labeled incorrect examples and the no-imitation instruction for this planned seed. Across 16 deterministic alternate seeds, all 16 transfer binders select the failure record and retain zero historical probe overlap; acquisition binders select it for 5 of 16 seeds. The preregistered acquisition probes have one exact overlap with historical input 3, so acquisition performance would not be an independent held-out measure; the transfer probes are disjoint. No inference was performed, so model attention and developmental improvement remain open experimental questions. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000011/plan_v1.json), [verified owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000011/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [outcome report](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000011/negative_history_policy_retrieval_outcome_v3.json).


Campaign 13 also removes the seed-boundary dead end for sequential preflights. Cycle index 0 still has to match the exact preregistered initial history, recovery, and admission boundaries. For later cycles, preflight now requires the supplied root to be the immediately preceding planned cycle, then verifies the completed plan prefix is append-only across history files, campaign bindings, the runtime admission ledger, and cognition recovery state before building a prompt. The cycle 0 plan and CPU preflight verify. The prefix verifier also passed against the existing completed cycle 09 and reproduced the new campaign's seed boundary. End-to-end cycle-index 1 preflight remains pending until planned cycle 29 exists; no model inference was run. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000013/plan_v1.json), [verified cycle 0 preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000013/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [prefix audit outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000013/sequential_history_prefix_preflight_outcome_v1.json).


Campaign 14 is a fresh sequential plan using an exploratory seed selected from campaign 13's 64-seed CPU screen. Its verified CPU preflight binds the cycle 0 0/3 score record into both acquisition and transfer prompts and verifies the no-imitation instruction in each. One acquisition probe (4) overlaps the historical negative examples; no screened seed made that acquisition overlap zero. The planned transfer probes have zero overlap, and the prompts remain within their bound. The plan and initial history boundary verify, but model inference and same-plan cycle 1 preflight have not run. The seed was selected post hoc from owner-only CPU evidence, so its timing is not independently attested. See the [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000014/plan_v1.json), [verified owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000014/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), and [seed follow-up outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000014/negative_history_seed_followup_outcome_v1.json).


## 2026-11-24: Negative-example ablation and sequential follow-up

The CPU owner preflight now builds a matched prompt reconstruction that removes only `prior_incorrect_examples` from selected history packets. It verifies that the no-imitation instruction remains in the baseline and ablated prompt, preserves selected record IDs and digests, and records the removed-evidence digest, prompt sizes, and held-out probes. Campaign 17 reconstructed this pair for both cycles. Each selected packet carried three projected negative examples; removing it shortened the prompt by 1,495 characters in cycle 0 and 418 characters in cycle 1. This remains an offline diagnostic, not a model condition.

The first Hermes launch exposed a compatibility bug before inference: recovery recognized the persisted task-family `...evaluator_correct_coverage...:v9` policy as valid but did not classify it as family-bound. The family validator now accepts that existing policy. The failed attempt is retained separately as campaign 16 cycle 0; campaign 17 used fresh cycle IDs and the same unchanged initial history boundary.

Campaign 17 cycle 0 scored 3/3 in acquisition under history-present, withheld, and restored conditions, then scored 0/3 on held-out transfer in all three conditions. Its transfer record became the selected historical source for cycle 1. The cycle 1 preflight verified that the same record and its three negative examples would be delivered, with three disjoint transfer probes reserved. Hermes then produced a `selective` acquisition result that was ineligible for developmental writeback, so the transfer stage did not run. The full-chain audit therefore rejected the completed cycle as lacking a retained eligible acquisition record. A separate cycle 0 prefix audit verified history append-only continuity, seed boundaries, runtime admission, and cognition recovery state.

These results establish that the failure record can be retrieved and projected into a later prompt. They do not establish model attention, a causal negative-example effect, successful learning, or improvement. The first cycle's perfect acquisition and failed held-out transfer are a reminder that acquisition performance alone is not evidence of transfer. The [negative-example ablation schema](rootless_planned_cycle_negative_example_ablation.schema.json), [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000017/plan_v1.json), [cycle 0 preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000017/owner_preflight_cycle0_v1.json), [cycle 1 preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000017/owner_preflight_cycle1_v1.json), [verified cycle 0 prefix audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000017/cycle0_prefix_audit_v1.json), and [campaign outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000017/negative_history_followup_outcome_v1.json) preserve the evidence.

### Strategy preview confidence-effect projection compatibility (2026-11-24)

The historical-examples pair preflight exposed a preview-only mismatch: selected
task-family packets carried historical confidence-history effects, but the
objective strategy preview projected them under a schema that omitted those
effects. The preview now requests the same effect projection for v5/v6 task
families as the governed scheduled-history path, so packet contents and
projection declarations stay aligned in both strategy arms.

The fresh current-identity historical-examples preflight stopped because the
ranked history selection contained no raw `examples` arrays. That raw-example
pair cannot measure compact `prior_incorrect_examples` guidance. I added a
separate owner-composed negative-guidance preview path that removes only the
compact incorrect-example packet, rebuilds its history projection and guidance
receipt, and checks that the no-imitation instruction, selected records, and
per-record strategies remain bound. CPU previews for seeds 151 and 153 both
selected the same score-bound transfer failure record and removed its three
incorrect examples. Both independently reconstruct with disjoint held-out
probes. The previews establish exact prompt composition only: no runtime
condition, model invocation, attention, learning, or improvement was observed.
The [seed 151 protocol and preview](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/preview_151_v5.json)
and [verification](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/verification_151_v5b.json),
and [seed 153 protocol and preview](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/preview_153_v5.json)
and [verification](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/verification_153_v5.json)
freeze and independently reconstruct the task, answer-key commitment,
generation settings, probe audit, exact prompts, and resident one-shot arm
bindings. The owner now has a protocol-gated execution method and history
inventory adapter for these distinct negative-guidance arms. The append-only
pair coordinator persists one-shot claims, verified receipts, the exact
owner-verified cognition observation and its digest, including the observation's
receipt ID, receipt digest, output digest, frozen tick, and request binding.
It also persists stable runtime history inventories, reconstructed paired
scores, and a terminal outcome. Reloading a completed pair rechecks those
bindings; a claimed arm without a durable result
is treated as consumed and is not retried. The score remains descriptive:
independent scorer attestation, learning, and improvement are not established.
The score now records paired correctness transitions, the omitted-minus-present
correct-count difference, and an exact two-sided McNemar probability as a
reduced integer fraction. Abstentions count as not correct in this paired
correctness comparison; coverage remains separate. The statistic is reported
descriptively and makes no inferential claim, especially given the small probe
suite. The first complete runtime pair and its reloaded journal verification are
recorded below. The failed raw-example preflight remains documented in the
[current-identity outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/historical-examples-negative-replication-20261124/current_identity_preflight_outcome_v1.json).

The rootless Ollama cycle runner now accepts this frozen preview through
`SENTIENTOS_HERMES_NEGATIVE_GUIDANCE_PREVIEW_PATH` and its private task-plan
store through `SENTIENTOS_HERMES_NEGATIVE_GUIDANCE_JOURNAL_ROOT`. Set
`SENTIENTOS_HERMES_PREREGISTRATION_DIGEST` to the protocol digest's 64
lowercase hexadecimal characters (without the `sha256:` prefix), and set
`SENTIENTOS_ROOTLESS_PRIOR_HISTORY_ROOT` to the source cycle's resident
`history` directory. It reloads
the source receipt and private task, reconstructs the protocol, predicts and
checks model identity before allocating the run root or loading Ollama, checks
GPU headroom before the one-shot slot, seeds the exact prior history, then
rebuilds the owner prompt pair. The runtime binding may refresh only the
session-specific owner protocol and request-envelope digests; task, history,
generation budget, ablation, probe bindings, and both exact invocation prompt
digests must remain identical to the frozen preflight. This binds the current
session before the first inference without treating old session linkage as
current. Both resident ticks execute through the durable pair journal under the existing
per-call resource gates. The runner records source-history, resource-gate,
model-identity, unload, and pair-terminal bindings. The first completed runtime pair used seed 167. Its [frozen preview](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/preview_167_v1.json)
and [CPU verification](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/verification_167_v1.json)
bind the task, answer-key commitment, selected history, disjoint held-out probes,
generation configuration, and both exact prompts. The [execution summary](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/execution_seed167_v1.json)
records runtime protocol `sha256:26f373f121b3e348c249995ad7c8e4445e1a882d7e03276d6c529f72c8d33f68`;
a fresh process reconstructed the journal score and terminal, and verified both
owner observations and inference receipts. The score was 0/3 correct in both
arms. Coverage was 3/3 with negative examples present and 1/3 with them omitted.
All three paired probes were incorrect in both arms. This single three-probe
pair establishes neither a causal effect nor learning or improvement; scoring
uses a same-process private answer key and is not independently attested.

A separately implemented [shadow scorer](../../scripts/verify_negative_guidance_pair_shadow_score.py)
re-parses both persisted outputs, rebuilds the committed answer key from the
private task, and independently recomputes correctness, coverage, confidence
error, and paired transitions. Its [seed 167 report](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/shadow_score_seed167_v1.json)
matches the journal's correctness, coverage, confidence, and paired-transition
fields. It stores no raw model output. This second implementation shares the
same machine, private task custody, and input receipts; it reduces single-code-path
risk but does not constitute an independent scorer attestation.
Hermes was unloaded after execution and its residency check reported it absent.

Five earlier one-shot launches on seeds 157, 159, 161, 163, and 165 stopped at
successive receipt, observation, or score-persistence bindings. Their runs remain
incomplete, and none of their arms was retried under the same protocol. Those
failures drove four corrections: fetch receipts before validating observations,
bind the frozen generation digest to the actual structured-output invocation,
reconstruct observation digests without the observation ID, and include the
negative-guidance arm in the resident score protocol identity. The runtime
projection also now carries the same confidence-history effects as the preview.
The runner uses the preview owner's 32-fact / 8-record selection limits, source
kinds reconstructed from the seeded history, and no history-order trial
override, matching the CPU preview configuration. Resource samples are
host-wide and do not attribute GPU memory to this process, so unload evidence is
reported separately from process-specific GPU accounting.

### Acquisition writeback readiness diagnostics (2026-11-24)

Campaign 17 showed that a `selective` acquisition was discarded before any
history record could preserve its answerable probes and abstentions. The cycle
runner now admits a complete, correctly aligned score vector when every probe
has either a typed answer with an evaluator Boolean or an explicit abstention
with null answer/correctness, and the score schema and aggregate counts agree.
Existing task-family projection code retains abstentions as correctness-unknown
examples. This makes the episode durable without treating abstention as an
error or as evidence of competence. Transfer still requires the existing
minimum of two accurate acquisition examples and a qualified history-withheld
baseline; selective scores with insufficient accuracy are retained but do not
trigger transfer. The runner also records answer-free readiness diagnostics
with row counts and blocker codes. This changes retention eligibility, not the
learning or performance claims: neither follows from writeback alone.

Campaign 20 cycle 0 (cycle43) exercised the updated path end to end. All three
acquisition probes had typed, score-bound answers, and the durable history
projection retained three incorrect examples. The transfer stage remained
skipped because no accurate acquisition examples met its two-example threshold;
the history record was explicitly historical-only, and the run made no learning
or performance claim. This run confirms persistence of a complete score vector
and preservation of the competence gate. It did not exercise the explicit-
abstention row case, so that branch still needs a governed run with actual
abstentions. Although the plan reserved held-out transfer probes, cycle43's
recorded no-overlap finding is invalidated by the empty projected-record set;
disjointness from its seeded history was not established.

The campaign uses a frozen ext4 source snapshot because the Windows checkout's
Git metadata cannot provide a complete source-provenance observation inside
WSL. The plan pins the expanded runtime source set, including root modules,
scripts, work modules, and direct runtime packages. Cycle43 completed with a
clean, complete source fingerprint. Its campaign-wide boundary audit awaits the
second planned cycle. An earlier cycle41 attempt stopped after three governed
model calls when the source snapshot omitted `hf_intake`; it has no completed
cycle result and is excluded from outcome evidence. The expanded source pin
includes that package, and the corrected snapshot imported the chat service
before cycle43.

The cycle44 CPU preflight then found that campaign overlap checks were given a
persisted history-record envelope instead of its nested cognition candidate.
As a result, they could miss existing task-family examples and report an empty
projected record set. The runner's campaign preflight now projects the nested
candidate and keeps the envelope record ID for lineage. Campaign 20 cycle44 is not being run:
its frozen source pin predates this correction, so it cannot provide valid
evidence for the corrected preflight. The next transfer cycle needs a new
source-pinned plan and a fresh preflight against the actual retained history.

### Corrected history-boundary campaign (2026-11-24)

The [campaign21 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/plan_v1.json)
pins source revision `97a8f04aed6eede5ea926fcbaee3fbbf3e86bb4b`, the 2,517-file
runtime source set, and two cycles. Plan verification recomputed the source and
model identity pins before inference. Cycle45's corrected CPU preflight
projected seven seeded task-family records and found zero exact overlaps with
the held-out transfer inputs. The cycle retained three incorrect acquisition
examples; transfer was skipped because the history-withheld competence gate
was not met.

Cycle46's preflight projected those seven records plus cycle45's retained
record, again with zero held-out input overlap. Its acquisition score was 3/3,
but held-out transfer was 0/3 in history-present, history-withheld, and
history-restored conditions. The history-present and history-withheld answer
vectors differed, while all three conditions remained 0/3 correct. The
history-to-task-family lineage was verified, so this is evidence of response
sensitivity to the history condition without a correctness gain. The transfer
delta was zero, no history-associated decision difference was established, and
the cycle made no learning, performance-improvement, or real-consequence claim.
The two acquisition scores came from different task seeds and do not establish
improvement attributable to history.

A later answer-free audit of the persisted cycle46 score run independently
recomputed the score digests and per-probe transitions and validated all three
condition receipts against their requests, projections, and developmental
session binding. Its [digest-bound report](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/historical_score_audit_cycle46_v1.json)
has digest
`sha256:461ed6bd1bae2898799f1efaacabda26c374ca825ff1837ce1ad60a979b3fc64`.
History-present and restored each answered all three probes incorrectly;
history-withheld answered two incorrectly and abstained on the third. Across
the probes, two had no correctness change and one was unscoreable for the
correctness contrast. History-present was answerable on that third probe while
withheld abstained, so the observed coverage difference brought no correctness
gain. Restoration matched history-present on all three probes. This is a
single same-model historical association, not evidence of learning or a causal
effect, and it does not repair Campaign21's incomplete preregistered overlap
scope.

The [answer-free cycle45 summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/45_cycle_summary_v1.json)
and [cycle46 summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/46_cycle_summary_v1.json)
preserve the score and history bindings without answer values. The
[campaign audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/chain_audit_v1.json)
verified both planned cycles, the append-only history chain, and the seed
boundary chain (audit digest
`876e9bf618045ef4a62365bff00e7e09cd43099ec0c15908748e8cdddbd77452`). The
first audit attempt exposed the same envelope-versus-candidate bug in the
independent overlap verifier; that verifier was corrected before the successful
audit. These results validate history projection and boundary continuity, while
showing no transfer improvement in this two-cycle campaign.

A registered-family audit later found that the original v1 preflights checked
only target-family v7 history, although the transfer task also declares
low-range v6 history. A retrospective recomputation across both families found
zero exact overlaps for cycles45 and46, but it does not upgrade the original
preflight claim. Cycle46's 0/3 condition scores and history-sensitive answer
vectors remain valid outcome evidence; held-out status was not preregistered
against the full declared family set. See the [registered-family overlap
audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000021/registered_history_family_overlap_audit_v1.json).

### Score-bound selective retention follow-up (2026-11-24)

Campaign22's cycle47 exercised the explicit-abstention retention path. Its
three acquisition probes scored 0 correct, 1 incorrect, and 2 abstentions.
The source-run score binding verified exactly, and the persisted history
projection retained all three examples without promoting abstentions to
errors. The transfer stage was skipped because there were no accurate
acquisition examples. The same later scope audit found cycle47's original
preflight omitted the declared low-range v6 history family. Its retrospective
all-family overlap recomputation found zero overlap, without upgrading the
preregistered claim. Cycle48 stopped before inference because the provenance
Git override pointed at the Windows working checkout instead of the frozen
source snapshot. The resulting campaign audit does not verify a complete seed
boundary chain. The [cycle47 summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000022/47_cycle_summary_v1.json),
[cycle48 failure summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000022/48_cycle_summary_v1.json),
and [campaign audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000022/chain_audit_v1.json)
preserve that outcome. The [registered-family overlap audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000022/registered_history_family_overlap_audit_v1.json)
records the corrected retrospective scope for cycles47 and48.

Two replacement plans were evaluated before campaign25. Campaign23's first
candidate seed failed the exact historical-probe overlap preflight, so no
inference was run. Campaign24 passed probe separation but its first launch
failed model digest verification because the runtime default model tag did not
match the full tag pinned by the plan; the second slot was not run. Both
failures occurred before task inference. These plans remain unchanged in the
[campaign23](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000023/plan_v1.json)
and [campaign24](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000024/plan_v1.json)
artifacts.

Campaign25 fixed the launch bindings and completed cycles53 and54 on the
frozen v6 source snapshot. A later scope audit found that both original CPU
preflights projected only the target v7 family, omitting the v6 family that
the transfer task declares as a history source. A retrospective CPU audit
recomputed exact overlap against both declared families and found zero overlap
for each cycle, but that cannot upgrade the original preregistered preflight.
Cycle53 retained 0 correct and 3 incorrect examples; cycle54 retained
1 correct example and 2 explicit abstentions. Both source-run score bindings
verified, and both history projection audits classified the records as
projectable. The transfer stage was skipped in both cycles because the
history-withheld baseline competence gate was not demonstrated. Consequently
the experiment has no transfer comparison and establishes no learning or
performance improvement. The cycle54 strategy-inclusion preflight failed
closed with `task_family_strategy_ablation_candidate_unavailable`; that
preflight did not emit a source-family eligibility breakdown, so its failure
does not establish that the cross-range examples were absent or ineligible.
The CPU strategy-inclusion preflight now attaches a bounded diagnostic to this
failure: per declared source family it reports matching record/experience
counts and whether that family independently produces a candidate under the
same fitter. These standalone checks are explicitly not a decomposition of
the joint candidate gate; raw answers remain withheld, and the gate is
unchanged. The preflight CLI emits the diagnostic as structured JSON on
stderr. The historical cycle54 artifact remains unchanged because the new
diagnostic was not captured during its original preflight.

A separate [receipt-bound CPU replay](../../artifacts/experiments/strategy-inclusion-preflight-replay-20261126/cycle54_receipt_bound_cpu_preview_replay_v1.json)
on the current checkout did construct a provisional strategy candidate from
cycle54's saved history: 3 supporting runs and 7 distinct inputs across the
declared v6 and v7 families. The model was neither loaded nor invoked, and the
source history was not mutated. The plan-bound replay stopped at the frozen
source-implementation check, so this is retrospective evidence about the
current checkout and saved history; it does not validate the frozen campaign
implementation, revise the original preflight rejection, or establish
learning.

The [cycle53](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/53_cycle_summary_v1.json)
and [cycle54](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/54_cycle_summary_v1.json)
answer-free summaries and [chain audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/chain_audit_v1.json)
verify both planned cycles, their append-only history boundary, and the seed
boundary chain. The [registered-family overlap audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/registered_history_family_overlap_audit_v1.json)
records the retrospective v6-plus-v7 overlap recomputation and explicitly
leaves the original preflight claim unupgraded. The [attempt registry](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/attempt_registry_v1.json)
records the earlier failed launches and the [strategy-inclusion preflight
outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000025/strategy_inclusion_preflight_outcome_v1.json)
records the final CPU-only gate. The active runner and campaign auditor now
include every history family declared by the transfer task in their overlap
measurement; preflight schema v2 binds that family list. The next useful
experiment should freeze this correction into a new source snapshot and plan,
then recompute overlap before testing whether cross-range history supports
wide-range competence. The existing transfer path still requires the current
v7 acquisition task to meet both its accuracy threshold and its
history-withheld baseline threshold before the cross-range transfer request is
run. That keeps its incremental-transfer claim bounded, but it does not test
whether retained v6 experience can enable competence that the no-history model
does not already demonstrate. That question needs a separate preregistered
history-enabled-competence outcome with present, withheld, and restored
conditions, independent score-bound source runs, and exact all-family overlap
checks; it must not be smuggled in by lowering the existing gate. Even a
positive result would be evidence of history-conditioned capability in this
task, not by itself evidence of durable learning.

The active checkout now checks the runtime model tag and GPU-layer count
against the campaign's frozen identity recipe during CPU launch validation.
This guard addresses the campaign24 tag mismatch before model initialization.
It is a post-campaign source change: cycles53 and54 used frozen revision
`97a8f04aed6eede5ea926fcbaee3fbbf3e86bb4b`, so the guard has not yet been
included in a source-pinned experiment or exercised by inference.

### Three-pair preregistered follow-up (2026-11-24)

Before any inference, the [series plan](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_20261124_v1.json)
froze task seeds 169, 173, and 179, all CPU-verified prompt pairs, the common
model/history/generation bindings, and per-pair randomized arm orders. Seed 167
remains a pilot and is excluded from these totals. All three pairs completed;
a fresh-process shadow scorer matched each journal score. The [primary series
outcome](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_outcome_20261124_v1.json)
records 0/9 correct in both arms. Coverage was 9/9 with incorrect examples
present and 7/9 with them omitted. All nine correctness transitions were
neither-correct. Hermes was unloaded after each pair.

A separately labeled, post-hoc [response-dynamics analysis](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/response_dynamics_seed169_173_179_v1.json)
compared answer/abstention relations without retaining answer values. The arms
differed on 8/9 probes: six different numeric answers and two probes answered
only with examples present; one probe had the same answer. This shows response
sensitivity to the prompt intervention in these runs, without a correctness
gain. The [combined analysis record](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_analysis_20261124_v1.json)
keeps this exploratory result separate from the preregistered primary outcome.
Neither result establishes causal learning or improvement. The intervention
changes the prompt but performs no writeback, so it tests use of retained
negative evidence rather than developmental learning across experience.

A read-only cross-report verifier now binds the frozen series plan, primary
outcome, post-hoc response-dynamics file, and combined analysis by digest. It
joins each response relation to the scored probe identity, reconciles answered
and abstained counts with the primary coverage totals, and checks correctness
counts separately. The initial [cross-report verification](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/analysis_verification_v2.json)
reconciled three pairs and nine probe identities with primary coverage (9
answers versus 7) and correctness (0/9 in both arms), but left answer-value
relations source-reported. The enhanced [receipt-backed verification](../../artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/analysis_verification_v4.json)
reopens the three append-only pair journals, validates both arm receipts and
their request and prompt bindings, and recomputes same-answer versus
different-answer relations in memory without persisting answer values. It
reproduces six different-answer probes, one same-answer probe, and two
present-only answers. The verifier checks these relations against the probe
identities and receipt bindings in the exploratory dynamics report. The v2
[verification schema](negative_guidance_replication_series_analysis_verification.schema.json)
closes the artifact shape; the receipt carries a self-digest
`sha256:850a6985189d0bcec600575cc17d11451193875bd071a99b485a902c6d0ca740`.
The schema and digest were checked against the persisted artifact. This improves
auditability of the response-change summary; it does not rescore correctness,
attest scorer independence, or establish causality, improvement, or learning.
The journal remains the private source of raw model outputs.

To repeat the receipt-backed verification from WSL, point `--journal-root` at
the experiment journal parent (the directory containing
`developmental_experiments`):

```bash
python3 scripts/verify_negative_guidance_replication_series_analysis.py \
  --plan artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_20261124_v1.json \
  --outcome artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_outcome_20261124_v1.json \
  --dynamics artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/response_dynamics_seed169_173_179_v1.json \
  --analysis artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/negative-guidance-replication-series_analysis_20261124_v1.json \
  --journal-root "$HOME/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/sentientos-data/rootless-experimental/historical-examples-negative-replication-20261124/journal" \
  --output artifacts/experiments/negative-guidance-prompt-preview-20261124/analysis_verification_v4.json
```

### Campaign26 all-family CPU preflight (2026-11-24)

Campaign26 froze the corrected runner and campaign auditor before model
inference. Its [cycle55 owner preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000026/owner_preflight_cycle55_v1.json)
projected 25 prior records across both declared source families (low-range v6
and wide-range v7). The three cycle55 held-out inputs had zero exact overlap
with selected score-bound correct, incorrect, or abstained history examples;
the overlap measurement was complete. This qualifies the inputs as held out
under the registered comparison, but is not a competence result. The preflight
records `inference_performed=false` and makes no learning claim. Two cycle55
launch attempts stopped before inference, first on missing `psutil` and then
on incomplete source provenance. Their outcomes are retained in the [attempt
registry](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000026/attempt_registry_v1.json)
and [failure summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000026/55_cycle_failure_summary_v1.json).
Campaign26 has no completed model cycle and is not counted as an outcome.

### Campaign27 all-family developmental cycle (2026-11-24)

Campaign26 did not yield a model outcome. Its cycle55 launch first stopped
before inference because the selected system Python lacked `psutil`; the retry
then stopped before inference because WSL Git could not resolve the checkout's
Windows `.git` pointer. The [failure summary](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000026/55_cycle_failure_summary_v1.json)
and [attempt registry](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000026/attempt_registry_v1.json)
retain those attempts. The cycle56 preflight also confirmed that the next slot
must use cycle55's chained history boundary. Campaign26 therefore has no
completed model cycle and contributes no cognition outcome.

Campaign27 was preregistered from the same cycle54 history boundary after
selecting the installed SentientOS virtual environment and a Windows Git
wrapper that returned a complete source fingerprint. The [plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/plan_v1.json)
binds implementation hashes for 2,517 source files, the Hermes manifest, both
cycle identities, and all task/backend seeds. Cycle57's CPU preflight projected
25 records across low-range v6 and wide-range v7; cycle58's chained preflight
projected 26. Both measured all declared history families, found zero exact
probe overlap, and passed held-out input qualification. Per-cycle owner
preflights are [here](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/owner_preflight_cycle57_v1.json)
and [here](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/owner_preflight_cycle58_v1.json).

Both planned cycles completed with exact source-run score bindings verified.
Each scored 0/3 correct in history-present, history-withheld, and
history-restored conditions; each had 1 incorrect and 2 abstained acquisition
examples. The history-withheld baseline competence threshold was 2/3, so both
transfer stages were correctly skipped. The answer-free [cycle57](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/57_cycle_summary_v1.json)
and [cycle58](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/58_cycle_summary_v1.json)
summaries record the score bindings and gates. The [campaign chain audit](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/chain_audit_v1.json)
verifies both planned cycles completed, the append-only history chain, and the
seed boundary chain. The [plan verification](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/plan_verification_v1.json)
recomputed the model identity recipe and implementation map.

The full score ties across all three conditions provide no evidence of
history-conditioned correctness improvement in these cycles. The existing
baseline gate remains useful: retained cross-range history is not tested as a
substitute for competence when neither acquisition cycle demonstrates it.
Hermes was unloaded after each run. No learning, causal improvement, real-world
embodiment, or action authority is established. The next useful cognition
experiment needs a preregistered task with enough independent, score-bound
correct anchors to evaluate durable change, while retaining withheld and
restored controls and all-family overlap checks.


A separately labeled post-hoc [response-posture analysis](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/response_posture_analysis_v1.json)
of the wide-range acquisition stage found the same answerability pattern in
both cycles: history-present and history-restored were answerable on 1/3 probes
and abstained on 2/3, while history-withheld was answerable on 3/3. Restored
answerability matched present on all probes. The cycles shared one probe input,
so this is not independent probe replication. All conditions scored 0/3
correct. This does not establish that history caused the change, that the
model relied on history, or that learning occurred.

The offline [same-family acquisition classifier](../../analysis/same_family_acquisition_competence_classifier.py)
and [output schema](same_family_acquisition_competence_analysis.schema.json)
make that scope explicit. Applied post hoc to campaign27, the classifier
returned `same_family_acquisition_competence_not_demonstrated`: present,
withheld, and restored each scored 0/3. It also records one shared probe
between cycles, so a positive pattern would not count as independent
replication. The [analysis record](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/same_family_acquisition_competence_analysis_v1.json)
labels its timing as post-hoc analysis.

Campaign27 did **not** test whether low-range v6 history enables wide-range v7
competence. Each acquisition projection contains one projectable prior
wide-range v7 source run and zero correct examples. The all-family preflight
establishes held-out inputs, but it does not establish v6 prompt delivery; the
cross-range transfer stage was skipped by the unchanged withheld-baseline gate.
No evidence shows v6 examples were delivered in a scored transfer condition.
A distinct runtime path is still needed: it must preregister a
cross-range history-present / withheld / restored outcome, verify that exact
score-bound v6 history reaches present and restored prompts, require disjoint
transfer probes across cycles, and classify its outcome separately without
changing the incremental-transfer gate. That future outcome would establish
only a history-conditioned capability pattern; it would not by itself
establish durable learning or causality.

A new read-only [cross-range history projection audit](../../analysis/cross_range_history_projection_audit.py)
and [schema](cross_range_history_projection_audit.schema.json) make the first
pre-inference evidence gate inspectable. Applied to the cycle54 durable history
snapshot, it found 12 projectable v6 experiences with 32 correct score-bound
examples; 11 eligible source runs contribute eight distinct correct inputs,
with independent unique-input anchors available. The v7 comparison projection
also has independent anchors. This establishes only that prior score-bound
history can be projected for those family IDs. It does not establish that v6
history was delivered to a v7 prompt, that the model used it, or that transfer
occurred. The output explicitly leaves prompt delivery, overlap checking, and
capability evaluation false; no inference was performed. This audit is
post-hoc and is not a preregistered transfer outcome.

The next CPU replay identified a retrieval failure that the raw projection
audit could not expose: cycle58's owner selected four recent v7 records and the
binder produced no accumulated strategy, although the task declared v6 as a
source family. The [pre-change replay](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000027/cross_range_prompt_readiness_cycle58_v1.json)
records that result against the campaign27 implementation map. Multi-family
retrieval now reserves the two records named by a verified independent
correct-input anchor pair from each non-target source family when that pair is
available and fits the retrieval limit, then fills remaining slots with the
existing ranking. Same-family retrieval keeps its existing ranking. The new
selection policy is recorded as
`task_family_source_family_anchor_reservation_then_evaluator_correct_coverage:v11`.

Campaign28 freezes this retrieval change against the history accumulated
through cycle58. Its cycle59 CPU owner/prompt preflight selected two v6 source
records and bound a candidate strategy supported by two independent v6 runs
and six distinct correct inputs. The exact prompt projection verified, the
transfer probes `[543, 666, 871]` had zero overlap with selected history, and
the prompt was 23,499 of 24,576 characters. The [campaign plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000028/plan_v1.json)
and [cycle59 preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000028/owner_preflight_cycle59_v1.json)
record this CPU-only result. The plan pins the updated implementation; no
inference was performed. This establishes that qualified v6 material can now
reach the candidate strategy and exact prompt projection. It does not establish
model use, competence, or learning. Campaign28 still uses the baseline-gated
transfer outcome, so the separate history-enabled competence path remains to
be preregistered and implemented.
# Plan-bound cross-range history-enabled competence path

The next rootless cognition campaign can preregister a separate v6-to-v7
history-enabled competence outcome. Its owner-side gate requires at least two
independent, score-bound v6 source runs contributing at least three distinct
correct inputs, exact source record receipts in the present and restored
conditions, and zero probe overlap with every declared history family. The
outcome is serialized separately from the existing baseline-gated transfer
result. Baseline competence is not required for this path; a scoreable current
acquisition stage remains necessary to preserve the runner's matched task
context. Present, withheld, and restored scores describe a history-conditioned
capability pattern only. They do not establish learning, causal attribution,
generalization beyond the preregistered probes, or authority.

The plan schema and implementation are bound before inference. A CPU-only owner
preflight must verify the selected source anchors, held-out probes, prompt
delivery receipts, and implementation digest before a model run is considered.
The initial v9 plan draft exposed an identity-digest reader that did not yet
recognize v9; that draft is retained as a failed pre-inference artifact and is
superseded by a fresh plan after the compatibility fix.
The two planned campaign cycles share append-only history, so they are repeated
opportunities within one evolving history chain, not independent replicates.
Campaign42 was the first eligible CPU-only handoff: its [v9 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000042/plan_v1.json)
has digest `9a053007f9b8cd2d076558772a22b9d443e28690d002754985ed4098bcf13e28`,
and [cycle87 CPU preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000042/owner_preflight_cycle87_v1.json)
is eligible with the v6 anchor pair selected, six distinct correct source
inputs, zero exact overlap with all declared history families, and a 23,499 of
24,576 character prompt. The preflight audit digest is
`sha256:2feba60b811cb901f4a3489f9290e1c14abfa51484c5a7c3ac382c39229b7be4`.
Its cycle87/88 slots are superseded by Campaign46, so they must not be used
with the updated source.

Earlier launch attempts exposed two runtime issues. Cycle81 did not reach
inference because WSL Git could not interpret the mounted Windows-style `.git`
pointer within the provenance timeout; a Windows-Git provenance wrapper now
provides the required source receipt without changing the implementation
digest. Cycle83 reached objective acquisition but stopped on an undefined
local variable, which was corrected. Cycle85 completed three acquisition
condition inferences, then failed during acquisition-history writeback with
`experiment_history_binding_drift`; it produced no transfer outcome. The
failure diagnostic shows that preflight expected two records, while live
selection admitted those records plus `devrec-dec0f183ac5255063b0c87d8` from
four available records. Live selection reported no task family and used
`completed_tick_recency_suffix:v1`. The evidence establishes an exact-set
mismatch; it does not establish whether the available history or prompt
context changed between preflight and invocation.

Campaign43 factored retrieval-policy selection into the shared
`_developmental_history_retrieval_policy_id` helper. Its [v9 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000043/plan_v1.json)
and [cycle89 CPU preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000043/owner_preflight_cycle89_v1.json)
were valid for that implementation, but are superseded by Campaign46. The
preflight was eligible with two source records, six distinct correct inputs,
zero probe overlap, and a 23,499-character projected prompt. It did not
reconstruct the exact cycle prompt or establish model-generated acquisition,
attention, or learning.

Campaign44 briefly implemented the exact-ID restriction by filtering the
binder's input records. Review of downstream run validation showed that this
would alter `available_records` and break the protocol's full-inventory digest,
so Campaign44 is superseded. Campaign45 preserves the full record inventory,
verifies the frozen record IDs and digests, and makes the binder select exactly
that planned suffix. It fails closed if the expected IDs are no longer the
available suffix or cannot fit the inference budget.

Campaign46 adds a CPU-only replay of this exact-suffix path to the planned-cycle
preflight. The replay runs the binder once with its ordinary selection and
again with those frozen selected IDs, then requires the selected IDs, digests,
full available inventory, and selection metadata to match. This checks the
deterministic binding path but does not execute a runtime condition or establish
that the model used the history. The [Campaign46 v9 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000046/plan_v1.json)
has digest `af4a8d547ac9b49935048b2333ef93573226a10aa6274010c1fa237a4a7a28b2`.
Its source binding covers 2,517 implementation files; the implementation and
both model identity digests verify against current source and manifest. The
[cycle95 CPU preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000046/owner_preflight_cycle95_v1.json)
is eligible with six distinct correct source inputs, zero overlap across the
three transfer probes, and a 23,499 of 24,576 character prompt. Its exact-suffix
replay preserved the two planned record IDs, the full available-record
inventory, and unchanged selection metadata. The preflight audit digest is
`sha256:6d49194b4ce44889b6e78f413838fd62a5824d1dea63b10c144480ef38f81823`.
No Campaign46 inference has run. Cycle95 is the first planned slot; cycle96 is
an append-only continuation, not an independent replication. Campaign46
established exact binding at the binder boundary only; it did not run the live
resident owner or the three-condition campaign protocol.

Campaign57 first exercised the resident owner and binder through a no-inference
strategy-prompt pair. Campaigns58 through 60 superseded each other while the
condition-level preview path was developed; none ran inference.

Campaign61 adds `objective_history_conditions_prompt_preview` to the resident
owner and updates `work/preview_planned_cycle_runtime_owner.py` to preview the
actual three-condition cross-range protocol. The [Campaign61 v9 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000061/plan_v1.json)
binds 2,518 implementation files and both target identity digests. Its plan
digest is `dafad2008d89f71888e1286a9914a5593d161ff27ac405763abaa2787ee2e22e`.
The [cycle125 CPU preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000061/owner_preflight_cycle125_v1.json)

is `sha256:3f0b551f710f04d5f7233ebb4e1220e3b47f622b30610270fb559277969be453`.

The [cycle125 runtime-owner preview](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000061/runtime_owner_preview_cycle125_v1.json)

`sha256:806955236482820c0400c46407b59f97dc9606546395e2682ce2853437984d8c`.

Campaign64 carries the prompt-preview work forward with a persisted,

The [cycle131 runtime-owner preview](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000064/runtime_owner_preview_cycle131_v1.json)

Task probe inputs must match the preregistered preflight. The task digest uses

Campaign64 verifies the three-condition prompt protocol through the live

append-only continuation, not an independent replication. The initial history
boundary remains the cycle58 state; the next cycle still needs a verified
cycle131 output boundary. Model execution remains paused while the GPU is in
use. The plan records
`preregistration_timing_independently_attested: false`; no external timestamp

Campaign65 strengthens the persisted preview verification. Its [plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000065/plan_v1.json)
has digest `a2f1646fbd8e7c481acac2e2f7dc4282b1e1ec68c21dbed4b251799762b4c50d`
and binds the current 2,518 implementation files. The [cycle133 preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000065/owner_preflight_cycle133_v1.json)

`sha256:31f4b0f9c6b7c145b22222c8cdae2410b26c8ca04842d79c835dc8880a145c69`.

The [cycle133 owner preview](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000065/runtime_owner_preview_cycle133_v1.json)

It also confirms the owner payload states exact present/restored history,

`e5d283f112f3b3d0a65e464fa235cc62e55df2866ec3205ac1533d1a7f1ef7a1`.

This strengthens internal consistency and reproducibility of the stored owner
preview; it is not a cryptographic signature or an independent attestation of

Cycle133 is the first planned slot; cycle134 is an append-only continuation,

Campaign66 makes verification scope explicit and preserves older reports. Its

The Campaign64, 65, and 66 plan artifacts are schema v8. Although they preview

Campaign69 advances the plan to cross-range protocol v2. Its [schema v9

`b2918936da45ca00d068ccbb3d02c09b65ebbc333cafcf770a5eb878667f2772`.
Campaign69 binds the runtime's future outcome summary to probe-level
correctness transitions without retaining answer values. It does not yet

Campaign70 adds an offline outcome verifier for the future scored phase. Its
[schema v9 plan](../../artifacts/experiments/rootless-history-campaign-20261124000000000070/plan_v1.json)
has digest
`9f1a8612b9640868f34881610020ccf48a7c3fc54117cde23fa74a63090d40cd` and
pins the verifier with the rest of the 2,519-file implementation. The

`sha256:0ab586eb52d078b036b99d5a46fc86df9beb3facc17218be024f5706f6e09b82`.
Its gate is eligible, binds the same two source records as Campaign69, and
