## Model-free ordinary-chat novel-range transfer preflight

The sibling-history replay now captures a fresh ordinary-chat prompt for the
novel-range affine task family. Its three inputs (`1009`, `8009`, `50021`) are
inside the v8 family ranges and were checked against all projected parent,
left-child, and right-child history inputs, the earlier strategy-inclusion
pilot, and the earlier ordinary-chat probes; every overlap set is empty. The
answer values are withheld from the report and bound by commitment
`sha256:e606fd1931373bafc0fbe03282d7cec240bf4c90a23980963eca70f0db95d623`.

The same question and JSON answer contract were captured in fresh ordinary-chat
sessions for the common parent, both children, and an empty-history control.
The parent and left child selected the same historical record; the right child
selected its distinct record. All three history prompts delivered three
scored examples, while the empty control delivered none. The builder enforces
that each prompt is identical outside the history block and binds the cycle
boundaries, source rows, and implementation digests. Running the builder with
`--verify-report` reconstructs the saved artifact against the current archived
cycle state. The parent and left selected rows match exactly even though their
history-block digests differ, so this preflight does not isolate the left
child's added record as a causal treatment:

```bash
python -m work.preflight_sibling_chat_histories <cycles-root> --verify-report <report.json>
```

The [saved preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/later-session-novel-transfer-preflight-v3.json)
records the no-overlap result and answer-free prompt hashes. This is still
model-free prompt evidence: no response was generated or scored. A future
behavioral run must freeze a model-identity-bound execution plan and score the
held-out integer answers independently before it can support a transfer claim.

### Model-bound novel-range transfer execution

`work.run_ordinary_chat_novel_transfer` binds the same four history arms to
the pinned Hermes identity, exact fresh ordinary-chat prompts, generation
settings, GPU layer count, source report, and protocol digests. Its
`--preflight-only` mode creates a plan without invoking the model and refuses
to proceed if the projected history differs from the model-free replay. A
later inference run requires the plan hash and records the governed receipt
and raw response for each arm. It performs no in-run grading or history
writeback.

The separate `work.score_ordinary_chat_novel_transfer` utility checks the
governed receipts and frozen prompts, then derives the answer key from the
fixed task-family mapping and verifies its commitment before scoring. This
keeps answer grading outside the inference process. Even a successful score is
held-out behavioral-transfer evidence only; this four-arm comparison does not
by itself establish durable learning or identify a causal effect for the
left-child addition, whose selected parent and left-child rows match.

Use an isolated Ollama server on the loopback target and a fresh run directory.
The first command binds a model-specific plan; only after reviewing that
artifact should the same runner be invoked with its printed plan hash and
without `--preflight-only`:

```bash
repo=/mnt/c/Users/aprmu/Documents/Codex/cc-cognition-next
cycles=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/sentientos-data/rootless-experimental/developmental-cognition-cycles
install=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/runtime/ollama-v0.40.16
models=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-hermes-gpu-recall-20261126/models
py=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/venvs/rootless-cognition/bin/python
model_store=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/recall-factorial-ollama-store-20261126
export SENTIENTOS_OLLAMA_MODELS="$model_store"
export SENTIENTOS_OLLAMA_CPU_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:11435
cd "$repo"
"$py" -m work.run_ordinary_chat_novel_transfer "$cycles" "$install" "$models" \
  /home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/novel-transfer-preflight \
  --preflight-only
```

For the full run, use a new run-directory name, pass
`--expected-plan-sha256 <reviewed-hash>`, and omit `--preflight-only`. Score
only the completed capture with:

```bash
"$py" -m work.score_ordinary_chat_novel_transfer <run-directory> <score-report.json>
```

The first Hermes run completed on 2026-11-26 with plan hash
`3f223e9eac3bece9ea43c7d761ef20d6a6a0826f1ecb674d64dae2114d118fc1`.
Independent scoring found zero correct answers out of three in every arm.
Only the left-child response met the strict JSON-only format; it abstained on
all three probes. Parent, right-child, and empty-history responses included
extra prose. The right-child JSON payload included one numeric prediction, but
it was incorrect. This run supplies no positive held-out transfer evidence.
The plan, raw outputs, four governed receipts, source snapshots, and separate
score are preserved in the
[run artifact](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/hermes-novel-transfer-run-v1/).
The isolated Ollama server was stopped after scoring, returning GPU memory to
its prior idle level.

The follow-up [evidence diagnostic](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/hermes-novel-transfer-run-v1/evidence-diagnostic.json)
decomposes the result. Parent and left exposed the same three scored examples
(`2→5`, `4→9`, `5→11`); all agree with the fixed `2x+1` task mapping. Right
exposed one incorrect answer (`5→26`) and two abstentions, while empty exposed
no scored examples. A lenient extraction recovered one JSON payload from each
response, but none contained a correct target answer. Therefore response
formatting contributed to three strict-format failures, while formatting alone
does not explain the absence of transfer. The parent/left contrast also cannot
attribute an effect to the left-child addition because their selected scored
record and rows are identical. A better follow-up should balance the quality
of usable history across arms and measure generalization at several increasing
input distances, while keeping strict-format adherence as a separate outcome.
Recreate the diagnostic from the frozen run with:

```bash
python -m work.analyze_ordinary_chat_novel_transfer <run-directory> <evidence-diagnostic.json>
```

### Balanced transfer-distance ladder preflight

The next probe removes the sibling-history evidence imbalance and tests one
shared parent record against an empty-history control. It captures three
disjoint probe tiers in fresh sessions: near (`1001`, `1013`, `1097`), middle
(`5003`, `7777`, `10007`), and far (`60013`, `200003`, `900001`). Every tier
uses the same ordinary-chat question template; within each tier, the rendered
prompts match outside the developmental-history block. Each history-present
prompt selects the same frozen parent record with three correct examples
consistent with the fixed `2x+1` mapping; the empty control exposes no scored
examples. The preflight also checks the probes against parent history and all
earlier reference probes.

The [saved ladder preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v1.json)
is model-free. It does not create a Hermes-bound plan or run inference. A later
execution should bind the model identity, generation settings, and trial order,
then report exact-answer accuracy and strict JSON validity separately within
each distance tier. This estimates immediate transfer from one durable record
across ranges; it does not by itself establish durable learning or attribute
the original acquisition of that record. Reconstruct the evidence with:

```bash
python -m work.preflight_ordinary_chat_transfer_distance_ladder <cycles-root> --verify-report <report.json>
```

### Model-bound distance-ladder execution

`work.run_ordinary_chat_transfer_distance_ladder` binds the six history/empty
prompts to the pinned Hermes artifact and identity, generation settings, GPU
placement, counterbalanced trial order, source preflight, and implementation
digests. `--preflight-only` captures the model-bound plan and requires the
isolated Ollama service to have the pinned model tag but no resident model; it
does not generate a response. Full inference requires the exact printed plan
hash. Each response is tied to a governed receipt and verified prompt digest;
the runner checks that the history store remains unchanged. The separate
`work.score_ordinary_chat_transfer_distance_ladder` scorer checks receipts,
recomputes each tier's answer-key commitment, and reports exact-answer counts
and strict-format validity separately.

Example WSL setup and model-bound preflight:

The Hermes-bound preflight completed on 2026-11-26 with plan hash
`c6e8906af54fbb8d4b143a35bc42e0d541ff755ae9f72e90d026b92035c5135b`.
It binds all six prompts to Hermes 4 Q4_K_M, Ollama 0.40.16, and the 24-layer

The six-trial Hermes run later completed against that exact hash. Independent
scoring gave `0/3` in both history and empty conditions at near, middle, and
far distance; all three history-minus-empty differences were zero. Strict
JSON validity was `0/6`. A separate [payload diagnostic](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-full-v1/payload-diagnostic.json)
recovered all three numeric answers from the middle/history response, but all
were incorrect; the other recoverable payloads were all-null abstentions. The
near/history response explicitly described the retrieved record but then
abstained, despite its three mapping-consistent examples. These observations
show no transfer under this model and prompt setup; they do not establish that
the system can never transfer. The strict-format failure remains a separate
limitation, although lenient payload scoring also found no correct answers.

The [full run artifact](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-full-v1/)
contains the frozen plan, six raw outputs, six governed receipts, independent
score, payload diagnostic, and code snapshots. The isolated Ollama server is
stopped and GPU memory has returned to its idle baseline.

### Post-retrieval strict-format follow-up

On 2026-11-26, a second six-trial run added a compact JSON-only instruction
after prompt assembly and retrieval. The intervention was identical in the
history and empty arms; it did not change the retrieved record, examples,
questions, probe order, model identity, generation settings, or GPU placement.
The [frozen plan](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-strict-full-v1/frozen-plan.json),
preflight and execution bindings, six governed inference receipts,
independent score, and source snapshots are preserved with the
[full run artifact](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-strict-full-v1/).

All six constrained responses were valid JSON, improving strict-format
compliance from `0/6` in the unconstrained run to `6/6`. Content accuracy
remained `0/18` in both runs: history and empty arms each scored `0/3` at near,
middle, and far distance. Every constrained answer was `null`, including for
the history arm. The [paired format comparison](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-strict-full-v1/format-comparison.json)
checks that model identity, generation/GPU settings, questions, tiers, order,
and scored history evidence match. Thus the format intervention fixed the
measured output-format failure but did not produce correct transfer answers;
this experiment establishes neither durable learning nor that the model could
never transfer under another prompt or task.

The next useful contrast should test semantic rule derivation or a worked
example as its own treatment, applied equally to history and empty arms. That
would help distinguish failure to infer the mapping from failure to use
retrieved history, while keeping content scoring independent from format
compliance. The isolated Ollama server was stopped after scoring; GPU usage
returned to the idle baseline.

### Matched history rule-derivation procedure

The all-null strict-format result does not distinguish failure to retrieve or
use the examples from a conservative response strategy. The ladder runner now
supports a separately bound `derive_rule_from_available_history:v3` treatment
on top of the existing strict JSON constraint. After retrieval, it instructs
the model to infer a simple rule fitting all available developmental-history
examples and apply it to the probes. It uses null if no rule fits. This avoids
demanding mathematical uniqueness from a finite set of examples. The compact
wording keeps the longest strict-format prompt below the ordinary-chat 8,000
character bound. It does not state the mapping or require a visible reasoning
trace.
The identical procedure is appended to both history and empty prompts, leaving
the history contrast and strict output requirement intact. Plan schema v3
records the exact procedure and digest; independent scoring rejects a missing
or altered procedure binding. The scorer also independently compares the
model-bound history evidence with the model-free preflight and rejects scored
history evidence or a history block in the empty control before counting
answers.

The six-trial GPU run completed against this frozen hash. Its exact execution
command was:

```bash
export SENTIENTOS_OLLAMA_MODELS=/home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/recall-factorial-ollama-store-20261126
export SENTIENTOS_OLLAMA_CPU_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:11436
"$py" -m work.run_ordinary_chat_transfer_distance_ladder "$cycles" "$install" "$models" \
  /home/zombinator/.local/state/sentientos-cognition-followup-20261122-wsl/transfer-ladder-semantic-full-20261126-v1 \
  --strict-json-output --derive-rule-from-history \
  --expected-plan-sha256 f452f032326835fbb2e762bd5cee32520ee324a628040134c14aec22646bfa5f
```

The comparator requires matching model identity, generation/GPU settings,
probe questions and order, output-format constraint, and scored history
evidence. It also requires current v2 score reports from the same scorer source
that independently validate history/empty evidence separation. Both the
strict-format control and derivation run were rescored without replacing prior
reports. The paired comparison was produced with:

```bash
python -m work.compare_ordinary_chat_transfer_distance_ladder_derivation \
  <strict-format-control-run> <rule-derivation-run> <comparison.json> \
  --control-score <strict-format-control-score-v2.json> \
  --derivation-score <rule-derivation-score-v2.json>
```

It [confirmed](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-rule-derivation-full-v1/derivation-comparison.json)
that all comparison controls matched.

The derivation treatment produced `0/18` correct answers, exactly like its
strict-format control. In all three tiers, the empty arm returned valid
all-null JSON (`3/3` valid); the history arm returned fenced JSON followed by
explanation (`0/3` valid) and still answered all probes with null. The model's
explanations referred to the retrieved low-range examples but said they did
not support extrapolation, especially at larger magnitudes. The evidence
validator confirmed the same three correct examples were present in each
history prompt and absent from each empty prompt. Thus this treatment did not
produce transfer and regressed strict-format adherence in the history arm;
it does not establish durable learning or show that transfer is impossible.

The CPU-only model-bound plan preflight for treatment v3 completed on
2026-11-26 with plan hash
`f452f032326835fbb2e762bd5cee32520ee324a628040134c14aec22646bfa5f`.
It made no model invocation or inference and recorded zero resident VRAM. The

character history-prompt bound. Its [frozen plan and source snapshots](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-rule-derivation-preflight-v3/)
preserve the exact treatment. The isolated CPU-only Ollama process was stopped

is stored alongside, without replacing, the original score. The [completed

preserves the frozen plan, raw results, six governed receipts, independent

recovered valid answer arrays from all six outputs, but all 18 values were
null, confirming no correct answers even when ignoring the history arm's
fences and trailing explanation.

### Explicit-rule arithmetic and format calibration

The derivation run's universal abstention left arithmetic competence
unresolved. A separate v4 plan supplied `output = 2 * input + 1` equally to
the history and empty arms while retaining the same six probes, retrieval
contrast, model settings, and strict JSON constraint. This is a capacity
calibration, not a developmental-learning condition. Its CPU-only preflight
bound the treatment to plan hash
`24bd4e3e072d185ed29b6dadcde6fcb5bcb048da0d37ec277d8469e8ae67e4e5`, exposed
the same three history examples only in history prompts, kept every prompt
below 8,000 characters (maximum 7,957), and recorded no inference or resident
VRAM. The [frozen preflight and source snapshots](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-explicit-rule-preflight-v1/)
preserve the exact plan.

With the mapping supplied, lenient payload recovery scored `18/18` correct
across both arms and all distances. The independent strict scorer counted
`15/18` correct because the history/middle response wrapped its correct JSON
answers in a Markdown fence; format validity was `5/6` trials. All three
empty responses and the near/far history responses were valid JSON. The
[paired comparison](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-explicit-rule-full-v1/calibration-comparison.json)
confirmed the model, generation/GPU settings, probes/order, output constraint,
and scored history evidence matched the prior rule-derivation run. Its
[payload diagnostic](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-explicit-rule-full-v1/payload-diagnostic.json)
confirms the one strict-format failure still contained correct answers.

This establishes that Hermes can perform the arithmetic at all tested scales
when given the mapping. The previous failure therefore lies in inferring or
using the mapping from retrieved examples under this task prompt, with a
separate history-associated format lapse in the middle condition. It does not
show that the history caused the calibration successes, since the mapping was
provided to both arms, and it is not evidence of durable learning. The
[complete calibration package](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/balanced-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-explicit-rule-full-v1/)
contains the frozen plan, six governed receipts, independent score,
comparison, and payload diagnostic. The isolated inference server was stopped
afterward; GPU memory returned to its idle baseline.

The next useful transfer probe is close to the support examples themselves.
The earlier “near” tier started at 1,001 while the largest history example
input was 5, so even that tier required a large magnitude extrapolation. A
model-free protocol should first select unused adjacent inputs just above the
support range and verify disjointness from every prior probe; only after that
baseline should it increase magnitude. This distinguishes difficulty
inferring a rule from the additional burden of extrapolating it far beyond the
observed values.

The existing novel-range task family begins at input 1,001, so it cannot host
a genuinely source-near tier. The model-free ladder builder now supports a
separate `rootless_integer_affine_transfer_source_near_v9` family covering
inputs 10–1,000, with frozen progression tiers 10/11/12, 14/15/16, and
19/20/21. The nearest tier starts five input units above the largest support
example. These values avoid the documented pilot, prior retention probes, and
the full saved parent history. The existing wide-magnitude ladder remains the
default and its report is not rewritten.

The source-near [model-free preflight](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v1.json)
was reconstructed from the saved parent snapshot because the cycle directory
was absent. All 39 completed history records and their receipts reproduced the
previously frozen admitted-history boundary digest
`53ef022a87a544a6cce40ff303026d25e9f338686fad3cfba62aa8d7d697af1b`; the
preflight also binds the snapshot tree hash and the model identity plan. It
retrieved the same three correct support examples for each history prompt,
kept the empty prompts history-free, and recorded no probe overlap. The report
is reproducible with `--parent-snapshot` and `--identity-plan` and verifies
without inference. The parent snapshot lacks the original cycle result and
preregistration, so that provenance limitation is explicit in the report.

The report can be supplied to the runner with `--report` and
`--parent-snapshot`, then to the independent scorer with `--preflight-report`;
both bind the selected report hash and task family into their artifacts.

The pinned Hermes strict-JSON run used execution-plan hash
`34aed5acd735e975cc4dcce74cb3026bd166d9edcf3b98d13c96cf373baa05e6`. All six
outputs were valid JSON. Exact accuracy was `0/9` in the history arm and `0/9`
in the empty arm across the three tiers. The empty arm abstained on all nine
probes. The history arm returned `[15, 16, 17]` for inputs 10/11/12, abstained
for inputs 14/15/16, and returned `[20, 21, 22]` for inputs 19/20/21; both
non-null sets were wrong under the frozen `2x + 1` key. Thus history exposure
coincided with guesses in the nearest and farthest tiers without improving
accuracy. This single deterministic pass does not establish durable learning
or a general causal effect; each arm/tier has one trial. The isolated Ollama
server was stopped and GPU memory returned to the 1,140 MiB desktop baseline.
The [full run package](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-full-v1/)
contains the plan, raw outputs, six governed receipts, independently
reproduced score, and source references.

A matched rule-derivation treatment then used the same source-near report and
task, with the same pinned model identity, generation/GPU settings, questions,
trial order, retrieved support record, and strict JSON constraint. The
[paired comparison](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-derivation-full-v1/derivation-comparison.json)
verified all of those controls. It still scored `0/9` exact answers in both
arms. Lenient payload recovery found that the history arm abstained on every
tier but wrapped its JSON in explanations in all three trials; the empty arm
returned valid but incorrect guesses `[13, 14, 15]`, `[17, 18, 19]`, and
`[23, 24, 25]`. In its explanations, the history arm claimed there were no
historical facts, despite the independent prompt validator confirming the
support record was present in each history prompt. Rule-derivation wording
therefore did not make the stored examples usable and reduced history-arm
format adherence. The [derivation package](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-derivation-full-v1/)
preserves the separate receipts, score, payload diagnostic, and comparison.

The ordinary-chat reader now gives a compact field guide for retrieved
`rootless_integer_affine_transfer_*` examples. It identifies the recorded
input/output fields, says to use only distinct rows marked correct and not
abstained within one task family, and keeps the evidence scoped as uncertain
history without current truth or authority. Other history types retain their
existing guidance. An initial longer version made the assembled prompt exceed
the 8,000-character limit; the service attempted to digest it before checking
that limit and dropped the context. The reader now checks prompt size before
digesting, and the compact guidance keeps the complete matched prompt within the
limit.

The refreshed [model-free preflight v2](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v2.json)
was reconstructed from the saved parent snapshot. It reproduced the 39-record
history boundary and snapshot tree hash, retrieved the same three scored
examples for all history queries, and kept empty prompts free of history. The
model-bound prompts selected the expected record with
`history_context_posture=selected`; their maximum measured prompt length was
7,840 characters. The original v1 report remains an accurate record of its
original capture; v2 now verifies retrieval under the current renderer.

The pinned Hermes run used the same six strict-JSON trials and source-near
probes as the prior run. The independent [history-guidance comparison](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-prompt-guidance-v1/history-guidance-comparison.json)
verified identical model identity, generation/GPU settings, task and trial
order, scored support examples, empty prompts, non-history prompt, and output
constraint. Only the history prompt block digest changed. Both runs produced
valid JSON in all 6 trials and exact accuracy `0/9` in each arm. The new history
arm repeated the old outputs: `[15, 16, 17]` for near probes, abstentions for
middle probes, and `[20, 21, 22]` for far probes. They remain wrong under the
frozen mapping; the empty arm abstained on all probes. This single paired pass
shows that clarifying the serialized example fields did not improve immediate
transfer, and establishes no durable learning. The package contains the frozen
plan, raw results, six governed receipts, independent score, and payload
diagnostic.

The next projection experiment keeps each structured scored row and adds an
adjacent `human_readable_examples` view in the same task-experience object. The
view is deterministically derived from the structured rows, so correctness and
abstention labels cannot drift independently. Prompt validation recomputes and
checks the rendering before assembly. The preflight capture binds each rendering
to its record ID, source run, score digest, and task family; the independent
scorer continues to score only the original structured evidence.

The refreshed [model-free preflight v3](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v3.json)
reconstructed from the same parent snapshot and retrieved the same three
score-bound examples in all history prompts. It verified the readable line
`2 -> 5 (correct); 4 -> 9 (correct); 5 -> 11 (correct)` against those rows and
kept empty prompts free of history and rendering. The model-bound prompts
selected the source record and included both representations at 7,974
characters.

The pinned Hermes [record-linked rendering package](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-hermes-record-linked-rendering-v1/)
was compared with the concise-guidance structured-only run. The comparison
verified the same model identity, generation/GPU settings, probes, task order,
structured support rows, empty prompts, non-history prompt, and strict JSON
constraint. All six responses were valid JSON in both runs. Exact accuracy
remained `0/9` in each arm. The rendering changed the history guesses: near
changed from `[15, 16, 17]` to `[19, 20, 21]`, and far from `[20, 21, 22]` to
`[23, 24, 25]`; both sets remain incorrect under the frozen mapping. Middle
history outputs and all empty-arm abstentions were unchanged. This one paired
pass shows that the rendering changed immediate behavior without improving
correctness. It does not establish durable learning or general transfer.

The next evidence step was a seeded replication of the structured-only versus
record-linked presentation contrast. The exact source record and labels were
preserved across arms, with each seed pair reporting history and empty outcomes
separately.

The seeded replication protocol now has an explicit presentation setting in
the model-free report and execution plan. The runner also accepts a bounded
integer `--seed`; a matched pair must use the same seed, while separate pairs
can vary it. The independent scorer validates either no readable sibling view
(`structured_only`) or the exact record-linked view (`record_linked`). The
paired comparator verifies that the two presentation settings differ and that
the model, generation settings, seed, probes, scored support, empty prompts,
and non-history prompt match.

Six model-free reports were built from the same 39-record parent snapshot and
reconstructed byte-for-byte without invoking a model. They cover structured-
only and record-linked presentation for seeds 42, 101, and 202. The initial
[seed 42 structured-only report](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v4-structured-only.json)
and
[seed 42 record-linked report](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/source-near-transfer-distance-ladder-preflight-v4-record-linked.json)
were followed by the seed-specific v5 and v6 reports in the evidence package.
All reproduce parent boundary digest
`53ef022a87a544a6cce40ff303026d25e9f338686fad3cfba62aa8d7d697af1b` and
snapshot tree hash
`sha256:cda0d5e396366e7e2851b6a9fd77e6e7c132e2bab8c31b93b637b04cbc9608d0`.
Each tier retrieves the same scored support record and keeps its empty prompt
free of history; only the record-linked report contains a readable rendering.
Each seed used its own configuration-bound model identity; the seed changed
while the pinned model artifact, generation settings, probes, parent snapshot,
and support record remained fixed.

The three matched Hermes pairs completed 36 governed trials with separate
receipts and independent scores. All 18 trials per presentation produced valid
strict JSON. Structured-only history scored `0/27`; record-linked history
scored `1/27`; both empty conditions scored `0/27`. Record-linked presentation
changed answer payloads in all three seed pairs. Exact accuracy was unchanged
for seeds 42 and 202, and improved by one answer for seed 101: on the middle
tier, input 14 received the correct answer 29. Near and far tiers had no
correct answers under either presentation.

This is a limited immediate-response signal: the readable view changed how the
model used retrieved history, and it produced one correct answer in one of
three seed pairs. It does not establish durable learning, broad transfer, or
model-independent continuity. The [seeded evidence package](../../artifacts/experiments/ordinary-chat-retention-20261125/seeded-presentation-replication-v1/)
contains the six model-free protocols, six model-bound preflights, six full
plans and results, 36 separate inference receipts, independent scores, paired
comparisons, a machine-generated aggregation, and file hashes.

The isolated Ollama server on port 11436 was stopped after the runs; the
unrelated server on port 11434 was left untouched. The next experiment should
check whether the middle-tier hit recurs under new probes or a held-out task
family before treating the response shift as transferable knowledge.
